Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any engineering module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 766. The projective resolution has length at most 853 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 766 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the engineering structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 853. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
